- **snowflake-migrator-bootstrap**: First-run setup for the Snowflake to AIDP migrator. Finds or creates the one migration config file (snowmig-config.yaml), reads every field back with secrets masked, verifies Snowflake authentication by password, key-pair, programmatic access token or SSO, verifies the AIDP end, then smoke-tests the connection and reports the account, region, role and warehouse. Use the first time the migrator runs on a machine, or when any other stage fails with an authentication, connection or "no config found" error. ([oracle-samples/oracle-aidp-samples](https://github.com/oracle-samples/oracle-aidp-samples/tree/HEAD/ai/claude-code-plugins/oracle-ai-data-platform-workbench-snowflake-migrator/skills/snowflake-migrator-bootstrap) · ★61 · 2 copies)

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/snp-array-genomic-alteration-tabulation))
- **snp-based-cell-clustering**: Use when you have a 10x scRNA-seq BAM (possorted_genome_bam.bam) plus barcodes.tsv from a multi-person mixture and need to assign each cell to its contributing individual based on genotype SNPs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snp-based-cell-clustering))
- **snp-based-mixture-deconvolution**: Use when 10x scRNA-seq BAM + barcodes.tsv from a multi-person mixture must be separated into per-individual cell clusters and each cluster genotyped/identified (sex, ancestry, LR-based ID) for forensic purposes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snp-based-mixture-deconvolution))
- **snp-based-popani-strain-comparison**: Use when per-sample metagenomic assemblies of the same species are available and the goal is to distinguish within-species genomic diversity arising from point mutations versus structural differences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/snp-based-popani-strain-comparison))
- **snp-based-read-allele-assignment**: Use when long-read (nanoEM/nanopore) alignments span a heterozygous SNP within or near a CpG-dense imprinted locus and per-allele methylation frequencies are needed to test parent-of-origin methylation patterns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snp-based-read-allele-assignment))
- **snp-candidate-alignment-quality-scoring**: Use when refining a weighted SNP candidate table from Nanopore MinION reads: after the 20%/30% coverage and transition/transversion weighted-probability filters, to quantify alignment quality around each candidate via LAST reliability scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snp-candidate-alignment-quality-scoring))
- **snp-candidate-coverage-filtering**: Use when calling SNPs from MinION nanopore reads aligned to a target with LAST: coverage thresholds must separate genuine polymorphism candidates from mapping noise before consensus/probability reporting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snp-candidate-coverage-filtering))
- **snp-coordinate-annotation-handling**: Use when tabular coordinate data (SNP positions, peak intervals) must be converted into GRanges objects with metadata columns as preprocessing before range-based enrichment or bootstrapped null-set analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/snp-coordinate-annotation-handling))
- **snp-coordinate-liftover-between-genome-assemblies**: Use when curated SNP positions are given on the Gmax_a1.v1 assembly but downstream analyses (e.g., gene proximity or coexpression module assignment) require Gmax_a2.v1 coordinates, and Soybase VCFs exist for both assemblies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snp-coordinate-liftover-between-genome-assemblies))
- **snp-count-validation-against-published-results**: Use when re-running or reproducing the VADT filtering stage on per-tissue RNA-seq VCF files and you need to confirm the number of informative biallelic SNPs matches the published per-tissue counts before downstream ASE testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snp-count-validation-against-published-results))
- **snp-database-chromosome-splitting**: Use when setting up the MAPIT-seq reference bundle (GRCh38 FASTA, GFF3, RepeatMasker rmsk) and editing-call inputs require known-SNP masks per chromosome, or when FLARE/SAILOR needs chromosome-level dbSNP bedfiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/snp-database-chromosome-splitting))
- **snp-dataset-subsampling**: Use when the goal is to reproduce or extend runtime benchmarks of SNP pattern miners — i.e. measuring how running time scales with number of samples n and SNPs per gene l across HOGImine (k=1, k=2), SiNIMin, and FastCMH. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snp-dataset-subsampling))
- **snp-fragment-to-vcf-conversion**: Use when a reference-free variant caller (e.g. ebwt2InDel, DISCOSNP++) emits fasta variant fragments in KisSNP2 format and you need .vcf files to validate calls with hap.py against a ground-truth VCF. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/snp-fragment-to-vcf-conversion))
- **snp-gene-pair-interaction-testing**: Use when you have RNA-seq expression counts, SNP genotype data, a SNP-to-gene map, and sample metadata (including patientID) and need to test whether genotype effects on gene expression differ by infection condition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snp-gene-pair-interaction-testing))
- **snp-genotype-dosage-scoring**: Use when you need to rank germplasm accessions by resistance-allele burden against a pathogen from VCF genotype data plus a curated table of resistance-SNP effect sizes, e.g. to reproduce top-5 resistant accession tables for C. gregata, F. graminearum, F. virguliforme, M. phaseolina, or P. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snp-genotype-dosage-scoring))
- **snp-genotype-matrix-processing**: Use when you have an SNP genotype table with SNPs as rows and sample names as columns plus a matching microbial abundance table, and you need to identify significant taxa–SNP relationships via logistic regression in R. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/snp-genotype-matrix-processing))
- **snp-genotype-quality-filtering**: Use when a chromosome-split VCF or PLINK BED input needs quality filtering before population-genomic analyses, and genotype missingness, MAF, and LD-pruning criteria must be applied in a mandated order. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/snp-genotype-quality-filtering))
- **snp-heritability-partitioning-ldsc**: Use when GWAS summary statistics (here 114 published traits/diseases) must be tested for SNP-heritability enrichment in predefined gene sets — e.g., tissue-specific DE genes from exercise training — via LD score regression rather than simple overlap tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/snp-heritability-partitioning-ldsc))
- **snp-heterozygosity-statistics**: Use when heterozygous variant BAF values show outlier dispersion from systematic misalignment, incomplete reference, or site-specific sequencing noise, and you need to quantify how P-mask filtering improves SNP statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snp-heterozygosity-statistics))
- **snp-indel-variant-annotation**: Use when a VCF of called SNPs/INDELs (e.g., from GATK HaplotypeCaller after SelectVariants/ApplyRecalibration) needs gene-level annotation and effect prediction, as in JWES-based whole genome/exome variant interpretation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snp-indel-variant-annotation))
- **snp-indel-variant-calling-and-recalibration**: Use when starting from a duplicate-marked, indexed BAM aligned to hg38 and the goal is a calibrated SNP/INDEL VCF with QC metrics (SNP count, transition/transversion ratio) comparable to published JWES runs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snp-indel-variant-calling-and-recalibration))
- **snp-informed-genome-reconstruction**: Use when allele-specific expression analysis of F1 hybrid (e.g., CAST×B6) scRNA-Seq data requires a personalized diploid reference built from known strain SNPs/indels, so reads can align to both parental transcriptomes and multi-reads can be enumerated for EM-based weighted allocation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snp-informed-genome-reconstruction))
- **snp-matrix-molecule-counting**: Use when you need per-molecule SNP or RNA-editing fractions from an error-corrected molecules BAM (Sicelore step 4.b consensus output), e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snp-matrix-molecule-counting))
- **snp-null-set-matching-maf-and-location-bin**: Use when you have a significant sQTL catalog (sqtlseeker2-nf FDR &lt; 0.05) and need a variant control set to test whether sQTLs are enriched in splice sites, chromatin marks, or GWAS LD hits. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snp-null-set-matching-maf-and-location-bin))
- **snp-phased-methylation-track-inspection**: Use when a WGBS sample may harbor allele-specific methylation at an imprinting locus or known heterozygous SNP, and per-read, genotype-preserving visualization is needed (e.g., A/G SNP at MEST on chr7). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/snp-phased-methylation-track-inspection))
- **snp-phewas**: Variant PheWAS across three curated biobanks, Pan-UK Biobank, FinnGen R12 and the Million Veteran Program: one variant in, beta / SE / p / allele frequency for every outcome each biobank tested (nulls included), on one effect allele, every untested or unreadable outcome kept as a row with a reason, outcomes harmonized across the biobanks, and a replication verdict per shared outcome for every biobank pair and across all three. ([ClawBio/ClawBio](https://github.com/ClawBio/ClawBio/tree/HEAD/skills/snp-phewas) · ★1.2k · 2 copies)
- **snp-quality-and-boundary-proximity-filtering**: Use when you have called SNPs (e.g. BCFTools on reads mapped to predicted exons) and need to derive genotypable regions before comparing against a validated SNP reference set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snp-quality-and-boundary-proximity-filtering))
- **snp-taxa-linear-regression-results-handling**: Use when you have run mbQTL's linear regression (core MatrixEQTL model) of microbeAbund taxa on SnpFile genotypes and need to inspect the P-value distribution across all SNP–taxa estimations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/snp-taxa-linear-regression-results-handling))
- **snp-to-gene-mapping**: Use when per-tissue significant ASE SNP coordinate sets are available and the goal is to convert SNP-level ASE calls into gene-level ASE calls, or to quantify cross-tissue overlap at the gene rather than SNP level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snp-to-gene-mapping))

- **snp_functional_analysis**: SNP Functional Impact Analysis - Analyze SNP function: VEP prediction, variation details, phenotype association, and literature evidence. - **snpeff-annotation-parsing**: Use when the input is a snpEff-annotated VCF/CSV from the JWES pipeline and the goal is to quantify coding vs. noncoding variant burden and per-chromosome variant distribution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snpeff-annotation-parsing))
- **snpeff-variant-annotation**: Annotate and filter VCF variants with SnpEff and SnpSift. SnpEff predicts functional effects (HIGH/MODERATE/LOW/MODIFIER), genes, transcripts, AA changes, HGVS; SnpSift filters and adds ClinVar/dbSNP. Java CLI with Python subprocess integration. Use ANNOVAR for multi-database annotation; Ensembl VEP for REST API; SnpEff for fast CLI with pre-built genomes. ([jaechang-hits/SciAgent-Skills](https://github.com/jaechang-hits/SciAgent-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genomics-bioinformatics/variant/snpeff-variant-annotation) · ★394 · 5 copies)
- **snpeff-variant-annotation**: 当需要给 VCF 变异预测功能影响（HIGH/MODERATE/LOW/MODIFIER）、基因/转录本/氨基酸改变/HGVS，并用 SnpSift 按影响等级或等位频率过滤、叠加 ClinVar/dbSNP 注释时使用；做基于 Java CLI（可 Python subprocess 编排）的快速注释、过滤、字段抽表与可视化，产出注释 VCF、过滤子集、TSV 表与汇总图；不适用于需 gnomAD/CADD/SpliceAI 多库一次注释（用 ANNOVAR）或 REST API/VEP 插件（用 Ensembl VEP）的场景。触发词：snpEff、SnpSift、变异注释、VCF 注释、ANN 字段、HIGH impact、HGVS、ClinVar、dbSNP、de novo、功能影响 ([findscripter/everything-skills](https://github.com/findscripter/everything-skills/tree/HEAD/09-verticals/snpeff-variant-annotation) · ★11 · 2 copies)
- **snpeff-variant-annotation**: Annotate and filter genetic variants in VCF files using SnpEff and SnpSift. SnpEff predicts functional effects (HIGH/MODERATE/LOW/MODIFIER impact), gene names, transcript IDs, amino acid changes, and HGVS notation; SnpSift filters annotated VCFs and adds ClinVar/dbSNP annotations. Java-based command-line tools with Python subprocess integration. Use ANNOVAR for alternative multi-database annotation; use Ensembl VEP for web-based or REST API annotation; use SnpEff for fast command-line annotation with pre-built genome databases. ([sulfierry/mcp](https://github.com/sulfierry/mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/snpeff-variant-annotation) · ★1)

- **snr**: В эпоху агентного ИИ проблема не в нехватке инструментов — а в избытке переключений, черновиков, координации и фрагментации контекста. Среднее время на одном экране — 47 секунд (Gloria Mark, 2023). 96% компаний не видят AI-ROI потому что AI ускоряет production, а bottleneck переехал в coordination + rework. ([sergeeey/Claude-cod-top-2026](https://github.com/sergeeey/Claude-cod-top-2026/tree/HEAD/skills/extensions/snr) · ★9)
- **snr-based-peak-quality-evaluation**: Use when peak-picked and grouped 1D NMR spectra show suspicious peak groups (e.g., peaks from different metabolites merged), and you need to detect misgrouping via silhouette values plus SNR before regrouping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/snr-based-peak-quality-evaluation))
- **snr-based-signal-validation**: Use when after composite map peak detection has generated a full unfiltered peak list with SNR values computed for each candidate peak. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/snr-based-signal-validation) · 2 copies)
- **snr-bench-eval**: Evaluates the robustness of audio deepfake detection models under varying signal-to-noise ratios (SNRs) by testing binary (real vs. spoof) and four-class (real+clean, real+noisy, spoof+clean, spoof+noisy) classification tasks on ASVspoof 2021 utterances augmented with MS-SNSD ambient noise. Use when the user wants to benchmark on ASVspoof 2021 (DF), or asks about evaluating this task. Reports EER. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/snr-bench-eval) · ★3)
- **snr-check**: Check SNR (Signal-to-Noise Ratio) of content ([BizraInfo/bizra-data-lake](https://github.com/BizraInfo/bizra-data-lake/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/snr-check) · ★2)
- **snr-decoration-removal**: Use this skill when the question is *what to delete* — running a structured audit of a UI to remove decorative elements that don't earn their pixels. Trigger when reviewing an existing UI for noise reduction, when a stakeholder pushback says "this feels too busy" but you don't know what specifically to cut, or when polishing a design pre-ship. This is the operational counterpart to `signal-to-noise-ratio`; it gives you a checklist and a procedure rather than a principle. ([hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins](https://github.com/hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/HDeibler/universal-design-principles/plugins/perception-and-hierarchy-principles/skills/snr-decoration-removal) · ★1.6k · 4 copies)
- **snr-densification**: Use this skill when a design has *too much* whitespace — when the SNR pendulum has swung from cluttered all the way to sparse, sterile, or "empty-feeling," and you need to add signal density without adding noise. Trigger when reviewing a UI that "feels thin," "feels under-built," or where users complain "I'm scrolling forever to get to anything." Also trigger when designing power-user surfaces (admin panels, IDEs, data tools) that need higher density than consumer defaults. Sub-aspect of `signal-to-noise-ratio`; read that first if you haven't already. ([hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins](https://github.com/hashgraph-online/awesome-codex-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/HDeibler/universal-design-principles/plugins/perception-and-hierarchy-principles/skills/snr-densification) · ★1.6k · 4 copies)
- **snr-dependence-performance-analysis**: Use when you need to test whether higher SNR in some photometric bands yields a significant boost in MBASC (AGN vs SFG) classification performance, e.g. before deploying the classifier on shallower or noisier survey data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/snr-dependence-performance-analysis))
- **snr-detection-threshold**: Evaluates the detection capability of the Lunar Gravitational-Wave Antenna (LGWA) for massive binary black hole mergers by computing the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) of observed and simulated events against fixed thresholds. Use when the user has predictions and gold and needs to compute SNR. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/snr-detection-threshold) · ★3)
- **snr-emphasis-economy**: Use this skill when deciding *how* to spend emphasis — color, weight, size, motion — across a design so that each instance lands. Trigger when picking the primary action among many CTAs, when status-color usage has multiplied across the app and lost meaning, when the brand color appears in too many places, or when a designer asks "should this also be highlighted?" and the answer is almost always no. Sub-aspect of `signal-to-noise-ratio`; read that first if you haven't already. - **snr-gain-quantification**: Use when validating a deep-learning spectral reconstruction on simulated or experimental MSI/SCMS data where the ground-truth or high-resolution spectra are available, and the accepted criterion is a reported SNR gain in dB over the noisy reference using a small fraction of the acquisition time. - **snr-sample-size-representational-alignment**: 信噪比和样本数量调控神经网络表征对齐的方法论。研究神经网络潜在表征的通用性规律，揭示对齐与数据质量和数量的非平凡依赖关系。适用于表征对齐分析、神经网络可解释性、训练优化。触发词：表征对齐、SNR、样本数量、插值阈值、通用表征。 ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/ai-safety-eval/snr-sample-size-representational-alignment) · ★4 · 4 copies)

- **snr-writing**: Reader model and compression criteria for Japanese explanations, answers, self-review / acceptance write-ups, code comments and commit messages. Explanations are written for the requester, who prefers established terminology and asks about anything unfamiliar; comments and commit messages for the maintainer who opens the file or the log later. Use when the requester asks to explain a concept or a book chapter, answer a technical question, write a self-review / acceptance document, or write or rewrite code comments (JSDoc, inline comments) and commit messages, and whenever they say "S/N", "圧縮し… ([okayus/okayus-skills](https://github.com/okayus/okayus-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/snr-writing))
- **snrna-seq-data-loading-and-object-construction**: Use when starting a scRank analysis from raw snRNA-seq/scRNA-seq count matrices plus cell-type metadata, and you need a ScRank S4 object encoding species, cell-type labels, and a known drug-target gene before network reconstruction. - **snu-form-automation**: Drive live ServiceNow forms via the g_form bridge (navigate, set_field, get_form_state, run_ui_action, click_element): insert vs update verbs, optimistic-write verification, auto-handled native dialogs, and auto-filling mandatory reference fields. Read this when automating or visually verifying a form/UI page/widget. ([arnoudkooi/sn-scriptsync](https://github.com/arnoudkooi/sn-scriptsync/tree/HEAD/agentrules/skills/snu-form-automation) · ★56)
- **snu-procurement-doc**: 서울대학교 연구장비 구매규격서(별지 제3호)와 용도설명서(별지 제2호)를 HWPX 양식 그대로 작성하는 스킬. 제조사, 모델명, 수량을 받아 제품 사양과 경쟁 제품, HSK 관세분류번호, 정부물품분류번호를 조사한 뒤 kordoc MCP로 원본 서식을 보존한 채 두 문서를 채운다. 선택 산출물로 연구시설장비 심의요청서(표준지침 별표 4)와 붙임 유사장비 보유현황 검토표를 ZEUS 유사장비 조사와 함께 작성할 수 있다. 시작할 때 필요 여부를 묻는다. MANDATORY TRIGGERS: 구매규격서, 용도설명서, 별지 제2호, 별지 제3호, 장비구매, 연구장비 구매문서, 심의요청서, 연구시설장비 심의, 유사장비 검토표, 중복성 검토, ZEUS 장비 검색, procurement specification, equipment purchase document, HWPX 양식 작성. 300만원 이상 연구장비 구매에 사용한다. ([hyeshik/qbio-skills](https://github.com/hyeshik/qbio-skills/tree/HEAD/snu-procurement-doc) · ★28)
- **snu-reference**: Extended reference appendix: detailed file-structure notes, table metadata caching, agent best-practices, and edge cases not covered by the core. Read this when you need depth the other skills do not cover. ([arnoudkooi/sn-scriptsync](https://github.com/arnoudkooi/sn-scriptsync/tree/HEAD/agentrules/skills/snu-reference) · ★56)
- **snu-srnd**: 서울대학교 SRnD(연구행정통합관리시스템, srnd.snu.ac.kr) 브라우저 자동화 스킬. Nexacro Platform 14 기반 UI를 javascript_tool로 제어하여 과제조회, 지출신청, 참여연구원 관리, 카드청구, 연구업적, 구매, 출장신청 등 연구행정 작업을 수행한다. TRIGGER: 사용자가 srnd.snu.ac.kr에 있거나, SRnD/연구행정을 언급하거나, 과제조회·지출신청·참여연구원·연구비·카드청구·연구업적·구매·출장신청 등 서울대 연구행정 업무를 요청할 때. DO NOT TRIGGER: 다른 대학 시스템이나 일반 웹앱을 다룰 때. ([hyeshik/qbio-skills](https://github.com/hyeshik/qbio-skills/tree/HEAD/snu-srnd) · ★28)

Combinar com o protocolo LegalBR e conferir fontes oficiais antes de aplicar. ([prof-ramos/skills](https://github.com/prof-ramos/skills/tree/HEAD/legalbr/materia/ambiental/snuc-unidades-conservacao))
- **snuffy-efficient-whole-slide-image-classifier**: **arXiv ID:** 2408.08258 **Authors:** Hossein Jafarinia, Alireza Alipanah, Danial Hamdi, Saeed Razavi, Nahal Mirzaie, Mohammad Hossein Rohban **Published:** 2024-08-15T16:59:15Z **Abstract:** Whole Slide Image (WSI) classification with multiple instance learning (MIL) in digital pathology faces significant computational challenges. Current methods mostly rely on extensive self-supervised learning ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/nlp-llm/snuffy-efficient-whole-slide-image-classifier) · ★4)
- **snug**: A Snug app is ONE self-contained HTML file. It runs in a sandboxed runner, keeps its state through the runner's storage, and MAY think through the user's own agent at runtime — a chess game asks for a move, a tutor asks for feedback, a tracker asks a question about its own data. The user owns the app and everything it accumulates: both live in one file the user keeps, and the runner supplies the i ([snugprotocol/snug](https://github.com/snugprotocol/snug/tree/HEAD/packages/knowledge/prompts/skills/snug) · ★2)
- **snug-asset-handling**: How to reference user-supplied images, videos, and audio inside a Snug Remotion composition — Snug copies uploaded media into `public/snug-assets/`, and you reference them with `staticFile("snug-assets/&lt;name&gt;")`. Load this whenever a composition needs a user-provided asset, the user uploads media, or you are tempted to use an external URL or absolute path. Do NOT use this for asset URLs that are intentionally remote (CDN images that the user explicitly asked for). ([mellofordev/snug](https://github.com/mellofordev/snug/tree/HEAD/packages/scaffold/templates/remotion/.claude/skills/snug-asset-handling) · 2 copies)
- **snug-composition-patterns**: Recipe book of canonical Snug video skeletons — text intro, kinetic typography, image reveal, lower-third, product demo, end-card. Each recipe is a complete, working `compositions/&lt;Name&gt;.tsx` file you can drop in and adapt. Load this when the user asks for a kind of video that fits one of the recipes, or when starting a fresh composition from a vague brief. Do NOT use this for upstream Remotion API reference (use `remotion-best-practices`) or for the Snug project rules (use `snug-scaffold`). ([mellofordev/snug](https://github.com/mellofordev/snug/tree/HEAD/packages/scaffold/templates/remotion/.claude/skills/snug-composition-patterns) · 2 copies)
- **snug-preview-and-render**: How preview and render work in a Snug Remotion project — Snug owns the Vite player and the render command; the agent never starts the player or invokes `bun run render` directly. Outputs land at `output/snug-out-N.mp4`. Load this when the user asks how to preview, how to render, why a composition isn't appearing, or when you are about to invoke a CLI command. Do NOT use this for upstream Remotion CLI flags (those live in `remotion-best-practices`). ([mellofordev/snug](https://github.com/mellofordev/snug/tree/HEAD/packages/scaffold/templates/remotion/.claude/skills/snug-preview-and-render) · 2 copies)
- **snug-scaffold**: Snug's project contract for a Remotion scaffold — file-name = composition id, default-export rule, allowed metadata exports, framework-owned files (do-not-edit list), and how compositions are auto-discovered by Remotion's CLI bundle and the Vite player. Load this whenever you are about to create or edit a file in a Snug Remotion project. Do NOT use this for Remotion API questions (use `remotion-best-practices`) or for video-style recipes (use `snug-composition-patterns`). ([mellofordev/snug](https://github.com/mellofordev/snug/tree/HEAD/packages/scaffold/templates/remotion/.claude/skills/snug-scaffold) · 2 copies)
- **snuppy**: Snuppy — clone any live website into a pixel-perfect modern frontend project. Fetches sources, componentizes markup, ports styles verbatim, and verifies with screenshots. ([plyght/dotfiles](https://github.com/plyght/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/agents/.agents/skills/snuppy) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **snusfriend-audit-runbook**: Use when running a deep audit of the SnusFriend site or preparing a next-day focus plan. Provides the repo-specific order of operations for lint/build checks, live browser verification, audit scripts, docs-drift checks, and how to use Codex MCPs like Context7, Sentry, and GitHub during the audit. ([danielx171/snus-friend-shop](https://github.com/danielx171/snus-friend-shop/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/snusfriend-audit-runbook))
- **snusfriend-design-system**: Use when creating, updating, or reviewing SnusFriend storefront UI, page copy, or merchandising surfaces. Covers the site's premium-but-approachable tone, proof-led marketing rules, dynamic catalog counts, and the key data/config sources to reuse instead of hardcoding. ([danielx171/snus-friend-shop](https://github.com/danielx171/snus-friend-shop/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/snusfriend-design-system))
- **snusfriend-design-system**: SnusFriend's complete design system for building e-commerce UI components. Use this skill whenever creating, modifying, or reviewing any SnusFriend frontend component, page, or design. Triggers on: 'design system', 'brand colors', 'component', 'product card', 'cart drawer', 'strength badge', 'pack selector', 'UI component', or any request to build/style SnusFriend frontend elements. Also use when reviewing designs for brand consistency. ([danielx171/snus-friend-shop](https://github.com/danielx171/snus-friend-shop/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/snusfriend-design-system))
- **snv-accuracy-evaluation-against-ground-truth**: Use when you have simulated long-read (PacBio SMS) reads with a known variant truth set and need to quantify SNV calling precision/recall for a long-read variant caller such as Longshot. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snv-accuracy-evaluation-against-ground-truth))
- **snv-allele-observation-filtering**: Use when per-read allele calls at candidate biallelic SNV sites from long error-prone SMS reads (PacBio SMRT or ONT) must be filtered to phred-scaled allele quality &gt;= 7.0 before downstream phased genotyping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snv-allele-observation-filtering))
- **snv-annotated-reference-construction**: Use when preparing a truth-set diploid reference (two haploid FASTAs) from a reference genome plus a VCF, as the required input for diploid Linked-Read simulation with LRTK-SIM. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/snv-annotated-reference-construction))
- **snv-based-chimerism-genotype-assignment**: Use when post-transplant bone marrow (BMMC) or donor-mix scRNA-seq data are available as a Cell Ranger genome BAM, and you need per-cell host/donor genotype calls and mixture fractions from de novo SNVs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snv-based-chimerism-genotype-assignment))
- **snv-based-clonal-structure-validation**: Use when single-cell CNA clone assignments need orthogonal validation: paired single-cell + bulk WGS exists, somatic SNVs have been called (e.g. SCAN2/Mutect2), and an ARI between CNA- and SNV-based clusterings is the acceptance metric. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snv-based-clonal-structure-validation))
- **snv-caller-performance-tabulation**: Use when comparing the computational cost (wall-clock time, peak memory footprint) of SNV callers on ONT long-read BAMs, e.g. benchmarking miniSNV against Clair3 and Longshot on a Dorado sup-basecalled GIAB HG002 dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snv-caller-performance-tabulation))
- **snv-calling-and-haplotype-phasing**: Use when haplotype-resolved methylation or allele-specific methylation must be derived from PacBio HiFi/CCS modBAMs: per-read 5mCpG calls exist but reads are not yet haplotagged and no phased SNV VCF is available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snv-calling-and-haplotype-phasing))
- **snv-calling-from-bulk-wgs**: Use when a cell-mixing single-cell experiment needs a ground-truth cell identity ground truth derived from cell line-specific SNVs, and a bulk WGS sample (FASTQ/BAM) is available for one of the mixed lines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/snv-calling-from-bulk-wgs))
- **snv-calling-from-metagenomic-read-mappings**: Use when sorted .bam read mappings to a reference .fasta are available and the goal is sensitive SNP detection with per-gene localization and synonymous/nonsynonymous classification for microdiversity or strain-sharing analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/snv-calling-from-metagenomic-read-mappings))
- **snv-calling-from-ont-long-reads**: Use when you have minimap2-aligned ONT (or PacBio SMRT) whole-genome BAM/CRAM reads plus an indexed GRCh38/hg38 FASTA and need accurate, phased diploid SNV calls benchmarked against GIAB high-confidence sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snv-calling-from-ont-long-reads))
- **snv-calling-from-read-mappings**: Use when per-sample BAMs of shotgun reads mapped to de novo signature genes are available and you need SNV-resolution phylogenies or mixed-allele statistics to stratify MAG clusters below species resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/snv-calling-from-read-mappings))
- **snv-calling-from-scrnaseq-reads**: Use when raw scRNA-seq reads (e.g. SRA files from a GEO study like the Kim dataset) need SNV genotyping per single cell, and no matched DNA-seq is available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snv-calling-from-scrnaseq-reads))
- **snv-calling-from-single-cell-rnaseq**: Use when scRNA-seq data from a mixed-genotype or mixed-species sample (e.g. Jurkat/293T mixtures or transplant host/donor chimerism) must be deconvolved at single-cell resolution using sequence variation rather than gene expression markers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snv-calling-from-single-cell-rnaseq))
- **snv-callset-qc-filtering**: Use when raw breast cancer (GSE75688, SRP067248) or glioblastoma (EGAS00001001880) bulk WES and single-cell/bulk RNA-seq FASTQ files must be converted into QC-filtered somatic SNV read count matrices for Canopy2 input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snv-callset-qc-filtering))
- **snv-expression-signature-cell-calling**: Use when single-cell RNA-seq data from a tumor sample needs per-cell tumor/normal labels and copy-number-based calling is unavailable or unreliable (e.g., normal-karyotype AML lacking CNAs), or when validating CNA-based calls against SNV/expression evidence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snv-expression-signature-cell-calling))
- **snv-false-positive-context-annotation**: Use when benchmarking long-read SNV calls against GIAB truth sets and you need to characterize error sequence contexts (indel-proximate FPs, homopolymer FNs) or evaluate an indel-proximity filter for FP reduction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snv-false-positive-context-annotation))
- **snv-fraction-cell-genotype-classification**: Use when scRNA-seq data from a mixed-cell or chimerism sample requires per-cell genotype assignment from transcriptome SNVs, especially when no distinguishing marker genes exist or marker-based calls need orthogonal validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snv-fraction-cell-genotype-classification))
- **snv-gene-expression-linkage**: Use when you have per-cell SNV calls (e.g. .vcf-derived binary matrix) coupled with single-cell gene expression counts and need SNV-derived features that link genotype to expression phenotypes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snv-gene-expression-linkage))
- **snv-gene-linkage-modeling-with-lasso**: Use when you have a binary SNV matrix (n_cells x n_SNVs) and a gene expression matrix (n_cells x n_genes) from the same single cells and need to infer which eeSNVs are associated with expression of particular genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snv-gene-linkage-modeling-with-lasso))
- **snv-indel-precision-recall-evaluation**: Use when you need to quantify accuracy of germline SNV/indel calls (VCF) against a GIAB truth set, e.g. comparing a variant caller's performance on HG001/NA12878 using genotype-aware TP/FP/FN classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snv-indel-precision-recall-evaluation))
- **snv-phase-block-chaining**: Use when ONT reads with MM/ML methylation tags have already been SNV-phased (HapCUT2/WhatsHap) into fragmented phase blocks and you need to bridge adjacent blocks using allele-specific methylation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snv-phase-block-chaining))
- **snv-phase-block-connection-verification**: Reference for benchmarking haplotype phasing accuracy when a method (e.g., MethPhaser) joins SNV phase blocks and you must validate each connection against a trio-phased truth VCF before reporting N50 or switch-error improvements. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snv-phase-block-connection-verification))
- **snv-short-indel-variant-calling**: Use when tumor WES/WGS and matched RNA-seq alignments must be converted into somatic SNV and short-indel VCFs as candidate neoantigen sources, with per-read-group parallel calling and Mutect2-learned filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snv-short-indel-variant-calling))
- **snv-variant-calling-benchmarking**: Use when comparing single nucleotide variant caller performance on ONT long-read data (e.g., Dorado hac/sup-basecalled GIAB trio BAMs or Pbsim3-simulated reads) and per-caller precision, recall, and F1-score metrics are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snv-variant-calling-benchmarking))
- **snv-variant-calling-ont-reads**: Use when raw ONT R9/R10 long reads (e.g., HG002) have been basecalled with methylation tags and aligned, and phased SNV calls in VCF form are needed before haplotype phasing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snv-variant-calling-ont-reads))
- **snx-build**: Build the ScarletNX runtime and recompile games correctly. Avoid the setups that build "successfully" into something that does not work. It covers the CPM.cmake dependencies, the presets for Clang only and the deploy into games/. It also tells when a game needs a recompile, and which warnings a plain build does not show. Use it when you build or rebuild the runtime, or recompile a game. Use it also after you pull or bump a dependency, or when a build result looks suspicious. ([scarletnx/ScarletNX](https://github.com/scarletnx/ScarletNX/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/snx-build) · ★350 · 2 copies)
- **snx-build**: Build the ScarletNX runtime and recompile games correctly, avoiding the setups that build "successfully" into something that doesn't work. Covers the CPM.cmake dependencies, the Clang-only presets, the deploy into games/, when a game needs recompiling, and the warnings a plain build doesn't show. Use when building, rebuilding, recompiling a game, after pulling or bumping a dependency, or when a build result looks suspicious. ([LuvAbyss/B3-Express](https://github.com/LuvAbyss/B3-Express/tree/HEAD/source/.claude/skills/snx-build) · ★138 · 2 copies)
- **snx-code-quality**: The C rules of RFC #142: strict ISO C99, no redundant state and indices in place of pointer chains. Also one allocation per owner, atomics apart, no stored pointers and one global struct per file. Use it when you write or review C in src/. ([scarletnx/ScarletNX](https://github.com/scarletnx/ScarletNX/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/snx-code-quality) · ★350 · 2 copies)
- **snx-code-quality**: RFC #142's C rules: strict ANSI C, no redundant state, indices not pointer chains, one allocation per owner, atomics apart, no stored pointers. Use when writing or reviewing C in src/. ([LuvAbyss/B3-Express](https://github.com/LuvAbyss/B3-Express/tree/HEAD/source/.claude/skills/snx-code-quality) · ★138)
- **snx-code-quality**: RFC #142's C rules: strict ISO C99, no redundant state, indices not pointer chains, one allocation per owner, atomics apart, no stored pointers, one global struct per file. Use when writing or reviewing C in src/. ([n-popescu/Inkypaint](https://github.com/n-popescu/Inkypaint/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/snx-code-quality) · ★2)
- **snx-crash**: Take a ScarletNX crash report, svcBreak or hang from the log to the mechanism that causes it. Use the symbolizer and disassembler on the code of the game. Use it when the game crashes (signal 11 or 7), calls svcBreak or fatal, or hangs. Use it also when a log shows "*** Crash", or when game structures contain corrupted data. ([scarletnx/ScarletNX](https://github.com/scarletnx/ScarletNX/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/snx-crash) · ★350 · 2 copies)
- **snx-crash**: Take a ScarletNX crash report, svcBreak or hang from the log to the mechanism that causes it, using the symbolizer and disassembler on the game's code. Use when the game crashes (signal 11 or 7), calls svcBreak or fatal, hangs, or when a log shows "*** Crash". Also for corrupted data showing up in game structures. ([LuvAbyss/B3-Express](https://github.com/LuvAbyss/B3-Express/tree/HEAD/source/.claude/skills/snx-crash) · ★138 · 2 copies)
- **snx-gpu-shader**: Write and optimize ScarletNX's own compute shaders (astc.comp, vic.comp, future ones) for what the GPU actually executes. It covers constant tables, bit tricks, uniform branches, fewer fetches, and workgroup and subgroup structure.
